i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; VECTOR_SIZE is chosen at runtime\n//   to roughly match the average number of non-zeros per row, which keeps\n//   both short and long rows efficient.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the __restrict__ out) {\n    constexpr int VECTORS_PER_BLOCK = BLOCK_SIZE / VECTOR_SIZE;\n\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (VECTOR_SIZE - 1);\n    const int vectorInBlock = threadIdx.x / j < rowEnd; j += VECTOR_SIZE) {\n            t += val[j] * __ldg(&vec[cols[j]]);\n        }\n\n        // Reduce the partial sums held by the threads of this vector\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; A group","+//   (\"vector\") of VECTOR_SIZE consecutive threads cooperates on one matrix","+//   row: the non-zeros of the row are read with a stride of VECTOR_SIZE so","+//   that the accesses of the group coalesce, and the partial products are","+//   combined with a warp-shuffle reduction. VECTOR_SIZE is chosen at runtime","+//   to roughly match the average number of non-zeros per row, which keeps","+//   both short and long rows efficient.","+//","+// Arguments:","+//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix","+//   cols: array of column indices for each element","+//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows","+//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication","+//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix","+//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation","+//","+// ****************************************************************************","+template <int VECTOR_SIZE>","+__global__ void spmvCsrVector(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ cols,","+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,","+                              const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t dim,","+                              double* __restrict__ out) {","+    constexpr int VECTORS_PER_BLOCK = BLOCK_SIZE / VECTOR_SIZE;","+","+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (VECTOR_SIZE - 1);","+    const int vectorInBlock = threadIdx.x / VECTOR_SIZE;","+    const index_t firstRow = blockIdx.x * VECTORS_PER_BLOCK + vectorInBlock;","+    const index_t rowStride = gridDim.x * VECTORS_PER_BLOCK;","+","+    for (index_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {","+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];","+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];","+","+        double t = 0.0;","+        for (index_t j = rowStart + lane; j < rowEnd; j += VECTOR_SIZE) {","+            t += val[j] * __ldg(&vec[cols[j]]);","+        }","+","+        // Reduce the partial sums held by the threads of this vector","+#pragma unroll","+        for (int offset = VECTOR_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {","+            t += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, t, offset, VECTOR_SIZE);","+        }","+","+        if (lane == 0) {","+            out[row] = t;","+        }","+    }","+}","+","+// ****************************************************************************","+// Function: launchSpmv","+//","+// Purpose:","+//   Launches the SpMV kernel with the vector width that best matches the","+//   average number of non-zeros per row.","+//","+// ****************************************************************************","+void launchSpmv(const int vectorSize, const int numBlocks, const double* d_val,","+                const index_t* d_cols, const index_t* d_rowDelimiters, const double* d_vec,","+                const index_t dim, double* d_out) {","+    switch (vectorSize) {","+        case 2:","+            spmvCsrVector<2><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);","+            break;","+        case 4:","+            spmvCsrVector<4><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);","+            break;","+        case 8:","+            spmvCsrVector<8><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);","+            break;","+        case 16:","+            spmvCsrVector<16><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);","+            break;","+        default:","+            spmvCsrVector<32><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);","+            break;","+    }","+}","+","+// ****************************************************************************"," // Function: verifyResults"," //"," // Purpose:"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

                     \\\n    do {                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                     \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                           \\\n            printf(\"CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Kernel: spmvCsrVector\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   CUDA implementation of CSR sparse matrix-vector multiplication. A group\n//   (\"vector\") of VECTOR_SIZE consecutive threads cooperates on one matrix\n//   row: the non-zeros of the row are read with a stride of VECTOR_SIZE so\n//   that the accesses of the group coalesce, and the partial products are\n//   combined with a warp-shuffle reduction. VECTOR_SIZE is chosen at runtime\n//   to roughly match the average number of non-zeros per row, which keeps\n//   both short and long rows efficient.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int VECTOR_SIZE>\n__global__ void spmvCsrVector(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                              const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t dim,\n                              double* __restrict__ out) {\n    constexpr int VECTORS_PER_BLOCK = BLOCK_SIZE / VECTOR_SIZE;\n\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (VECTOR_SIZE - 1);\n    const int vectorInBlock = threadIdx.x / VECTOR_SIZE;\n    const index_t firstRow = blockIdx.x * VECTORS_PER_BLOCK + vectorInBlock;\n    const index_t rowStride = gridDim.x * VECTORS_PER_BLOCK;\n\n    for (index_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {\n        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];\n\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowStart + lane; j < rowEnd; j += VECTOR_SIZE) {\n            t += val[j] * __ldg(&vec[cols[j]]);\n        }\n\n        // Reduce the partial sums held by the threads of this vector\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int offset = VECTOR_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n            t += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, t, offset, VECTOR_SIZE);\n        }\n\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            out[row] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: launchSpmv\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Launches the SpMV kernel with the vector width that best matches the\n//   average number of non-zeros per row.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid launchSpmv(const int vectorSize, const int numBlocks, const double* d_val,\n                const index_t* d_cols, const index_t* d_rowDelimiters, const double* d_vec,\n                const index_t dim, double* d_out) {\n    switch (vectorSize) {\n        case 2:\n            spmvCsrVector<2><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n        case 4:\n            spmvCsrVector<4><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n        case 8:\n            spmvCsrVector<8><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n        case 16:\n            spmvCsrVector<16><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n        default:\n            spmvCsrVector<32><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n h_reference.resize(numRows);\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), \n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n    }\n\n    // Perform SpMV i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Kernel: spmvCsrVector\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   CUDA implementation of CSR sparse matrix-vector multiplication. A group\n//   (\"vector\") of VECTOR_SIZE consecutive threads cooperates on one matrix\n//   row: the non-zeros of the row are read with a stride of VECTOR_SIZE so\n//   that the accesses of the group coalesce, and the partial products are\n//   combined with a warp-shuffle reduction. VECTOR_SIZE is chosen at runtime\n//   to roughly match the average number of non-zeros per row, which keeps\n//   both short and long rows efficient.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int VECTOR_SIZE>\n__global__ void spmvCsrVector(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                              const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t dim,\n                              double* __restrict__ out) {\n    constexpr int VECTORS_PER_BLOCK = BLOCK_SIZE / VECTOR_SIZE;\n\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (VECTOR_SIZE - 1);\n    const int vectorInBlock = threadIdx.x / VECTOR_SIZE;\n    const index_t firstRow = blockIdx.x * VECTORS_PER_BLOCK + vectorInBlock;\n    const index_t rowStride = gridDim.x * VECTORS_PER_BLOCK;\n\n    for (index_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {\n        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];\n\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowStart + lane; j < rowEnd; j += VECTOR_SIZE) {\n            t += val[j] * __ldg(&vec[cols[j]]);\n        }\n\n        // Reduce the partial sums held by the threads of this vector\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int offset = VECTOR_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n            t += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, t, offset, VECTOR_SIZE);\n        }\n\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            out[row] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: launchSpmv\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Launches the SpMV kernel with the vector width that best matches the\n//   average number of non-zeros per row.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid launchSpmv(const int vectorSize, const int numBlocks, const double* d_val,\n                const index_t* d_cols, const index_t* d_rowDelimiters, const double* d_vec,\n                const index_t dim, double* d_out) {\n    switch (vectorSize) {\n BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n        case 8:\n            spmvCsrVector<8><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n        case 16:\n            spmvCsrVector<16><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n        default:\n            spmvCsrVector<32><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0;
